### person.educator_deduction — This person's educator expense deduction
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"input": "person.educator_expenses"}, {"const": 0}], "test": "le"}, "then": {"line": "zero"}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"input": "person.educator_expenses"}], "test": "lt"}, "then": {"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"const": false}, {"input": "person.eligible_educator"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"line": "zero"}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": true}, {"input": "person.eligible_educator"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"of": [{"input": "person.educator_expenses"}, {"param": "s1.educator_max"}], "op": "smaller_of"}}], "op": "choose"}}], "op": "choose"}
```

Second:

```json
{"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"const": false}, {"input": "person.eligible_educator"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"line": "zero"}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": true}, {"input": "person.eligible_educator"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"of": [{"input": "person.educator_expenses"}, {"param": "s1.educator_max"}], "op": "smaller_of"}}], "op": "choose"}
```

Ruling: representation; i1040gi p. 93, Line 11: "If you were an eligible educator in 2025, you can deduct on line 11 up to $300 of qualified expenses you paid in 2025." Same formula: the smaller of the expenses and $300 for an eligible educator, zero otherwise; only the order of the two dispositive questions differs. Every candidate-unknown record lacks person.eligible_educator where the expenses are zero and the project rightly does not ask it (classm.f2441.1.deepseek, completed: project $0.00); every project-unknown record is a partial withholding person.educator_expenses, where the candidate's zero follows from eligibility false (classm.f8889.2.deepseek, classm.f4952.4.deepseek). No value differs. Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read; the reply's expression and the project's line are unchanged from fix round 3. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/person.json; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,014 partial records; every witness listed by a scratch rerun of tools/transcription/triage.mjs with the witness cap lifted): 3,056 of 4,724 comparisons agree; 28 project-unknown, 1,640 candidate-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 809 candidate-unknown.

Ruling: interface gap; i8889 p. 7, Line 6, Step 2: "Divide the refigured contribution limit from Step 1 equally between you and your spouse, unless you both agree on a different allocation"; Step 3: "Subtract the part of the contribution limit allocated to your spouse in Step 2 from the amount determined in Step 1." The reply changed: it now applies the project's spouse-rule filter (both spouses with a Form 8889, family coverage), so fix round 2's value kind (non-split forms) is gone, but it still sums f8889.share, the published field carrying the f8889.allocation_bad refusal, whose test reads household.hsa_share_sum, which sums this line: a cycle. The 2 completed candidate-unknown records are that cycle (stage13.hsa_agreed_all_to_you, both spouses), as are the refused ones (stage13.refuse.hsa_over_allocated, generated 468); the 16 candidate refusals are allocation_bad reached through the cycle on returns the project refuses (stage13.refuse.hsa_family_one_spouse, stage13.refuse.hsa_mixed_methods, generated 84, 141 and 154). The interface gave f8889.share_raw and f8889.share by id and type only, so the candidate could not tell the pre-check amount from the checked one. Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read; the reply's expression and the project's line are unchanged from fix round 3. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/person.json; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,014 partial records; every witness listed by a scratch rerun of tools/transcription/triage.mjs with the witness cap lifted): 4,702 of 4,724 comparisons agree; 16 candidate-refuses, 6 candidate-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 2 candidate-unknown.

Ruling: model error; i2441 p. 4, Lines 4 and 5: "If filing jointly, figure your and your spouse's earned income separately" and "Earned income for figuring the credit generally includes the following amounts. 1. The amount shown on Form 1040, 1040-SR, or 1040-NR, line 1z"; amb.2025.dc_line1e_attribution (owner ruling: line 1e is the earned income of the spouse whose Forms W-2 show box 10; with both, refused while a credit remains, otherwise shown once on the taxpayer's line 4). The reply changed in two places: its line 1e part now reads f1040.line1e (fix round 3's read f2441.line26, which line 1e copies when benefits are reported), and its self-employment part is on sc.result again (fix round 3's at-risk kind is gone). As in fix round 3, where both spouses' Forms W-2 show box 10 it adds the whole of line 1e to each spouse (person.h_dc_1e), counting line 1z's line 1e twice, which no reading of the register allows; no record in the sample isolates it (the project refuses such a return at f2441.attrib_gate while a credit remains). No value differs on the sample. The project refusals are cited Form 2441 and return screens reached through the line (f2441.line14_bad on classm.w2.1.deepseek and stage15.refuse.f2441.line14_over, f2441.plans_differ, sa.excluded_income on classm.f8889.5 and .5.deepseek; refused returns). The candidate-unknown records also include W-2 box 10 asked where the interview does not ask it (37 on completed returns; box 10 is asked with Form 2441): placement. The candidate declares refusals on person.se_screen and person.rrta for every person; the project asks se_screen (and, through the Schedule C, the railroad question) only of a person with a Schedule C, so every candidate-unknown record is a person with no Schedule C where the interview does not ask se_screen (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5, completed), and every candidate refusal supplies se_screen or rrta true for such a person (classm.f5695.2, classm.f5695.2.deepseek and classm.ssb.5 on completed returns; classm.carloan.2, classm.f8959.1, classm.w2.1 and generated returns on refused returns), an answer the interview does not produce for that person: placement (checked on every witness in the scratch rerun: none has a Schedule C). Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read; the reply's expression and the project's line are unchanged from fix round 3. The reply changed: it now branches on the published ira.anyone_covered (the applicable limit where no one is covered; zero where line 6 is zero; the limit where line 6 is at or above the threshold; otherwise the reduced amount), where fix round 3's branched on ira.lines2to6. The 31 value records on completed returns (and 37 on refused returns) are all project $7,000.00 against $0.00 on returns with no traditional IRA contribution at all (ira.any_trad and ira.lines2to6 false in both graphs), where the candidate's ira.anyone_covered is true only through its person.ira_cov plan reading (amb.2025.ira_covered, ruled there) and its line 6 is zero: printed lines 7a, 7b, 12a, 12b and Schedule 1 line 20 are $0.00 in both graphs on every one (checked on each witness in a scratch rerun; classm.f8889.1.deepseek spouse, classm.s2.3 spouse, classm.f8962.2.deepseek, classm.tcw.15), so only the helper differs. Project refusals are cited screens reached through modified AGI on refused returns (person.plan_screened, person.simple_excess, person.sep_excess, ira.cov_gate); the candidate refusals are other forms' refusals reached through its modified AGI on refused returns (sd.other_forms, retirement bad_code, scope.other_income, s1.uc_unsupported); candidate-unknown records ask plan of a person with no Schedule C on a return with a contribution (stage16.ira.i1 to i3; amb.2025.ira_covered, placement) or eligible_educator, plan_screen and se_screen through modified AGI; project-unknown records are the project's helper needing ira_work_plan on returns with no contribution, where nothing printed reads it. Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read. Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 98: "On line 12a, enter the smallest of line 7a, 10, or 11a ... This is the most you can deduct." The reply changed in form: the smaller of its line 7, ira.line10 and person.ira_contrib, with the person.sav_trad gate now inside person.ira_contrib (zero for a person who made no traditional contribution). But the smaller-of still evaluates its line 7 and line 10 for that person, so it still needs the worksheet's answers (ira_work_plan, ira_excess, plan, plan_screen, se_screen and the self-employment capital answer), as in fix round 3; the project gates the whole line on person.ira_has. Of the candidate-unknown records on completed returns, checked on each witness in a scratch rerun, 875 are persons who answered sav_trad no, where the interview does not ask those questions (classm.f2441.1.deepseek, classm.f8995.2, stage15.f8880.s2) (the other 26 are contributors, stage16.ira.i1 to i10, asked plan, plan_screen, se_screen or eligible_educator where the interview does not ask them: placement); for such a person the amount is zero whatever the answers (i1040gi p. 95, Line 20: "If you made contributions to a traditional IRA for 2025, you may be able to take an IRA deduction."), so the reply requires answers the source does not need to determine the result. No value differs. The candidate refusals are other forms' refusals reached through its worksheet for persons with no contribution (scveh low_use, s179.multi, s1.alimony_unsupported; refused returns) and its se_screen placements (classm.f5695.2 and classm.ssb.5 on completed returns); the project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (ira.both_kinds, ira.kbh_short, person.ira_over_limit, ira.over_comp, ira.special_gate, person.ira_dist_gate on stage16.refuse.*; p590a p. 35). Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/person.json; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,014 partial records; every witness listed by a scratch rerun of tools/transcription/triage.mjs with the witness cap lifted): 2,450 of 4,724 comparisons agree; 11 project-refuses, 270 candidate-refuses, 1,993 candidate-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 3 candidate-refuses, 901 candidate-unknown.

Same formula as the project's (the smaller of ira.line10 and its line 11, less its line 12), changed only in form; through person.ira_deduction it still needs the worksheet's answers for a person who made no traditional contribution (ira_work_plan, ira_excess, plan, plan_screen and se_screen); the project gates on person.ira_has. Of the candidate-unknown records on completed returns, checked on each witness in a scratch rerun, 875 are persons who answered sav_trad no, where the interview does not ask those questions (classm.f2441.1.deepseek, classm.f8995.2, stage15.f8880.s2) (the other 26 are contributors, stage16.ira.i1 to i10, asked plan, plan_screen, se_screen or eligible_educator where the interview does not ask them: placement); for such a person the amount is zero whatever the answers (i1040gi p. 95, Line 20: "If you made contributions to a traditional IRA for 2025, you may be able to take an IRA deduction."), so the reply requires answers the source does not need to determine the result. No value differs. The candidate refusals are other forms' refusals reached through its worksheet for persons with no contribution (scveh low_use, s179.multi, s1.alimony_unsupported; refused returns) and its se_screen placements (classm.f5695.2 and classm.ssb.5 on completed returns); the project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (ira.both_kinds, ira.kbh_short, person.ira_over_limit, ira.over_comp, ira.special_gate, person.ira_dist_gate on stage16.refuse.*; p590a p. 35). Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read. Ruling: model error; i8606 p. 2, Who Must File: "You made nondeductible contributions to a traditional IRA for 2025" and "You received distributions from a traditional IRA in 2025 and your basis in these IRAs is more than zero." The reply tests both conditions as before (the project screens a distribution with basis at retirement.special), but through person.ira_nondeductible it still needs the worksheet's answers for a person who made no traditional contribution (ira_work_plan, ira_excess, ira_basis, ira_dist, plan, plan_screen and se_screen). Of the candidate-unknown records on completed returns, checked on each witness in a scratch rerun, 875 are persons who answered sav_trad no, where the interview does not ask those questions (classm.f2441.1.deepseek, classm.f8995.2, stage15.f8880.s2) (the other 26 are contributors, stage16.ira.i1 to i10, asked plan, plan_screen, se_screen or eligible_educator where the interview does not ask them: placement); for such a person the amount is zero whatever the answers (i1040gi p. 95, Line 20: "If you made contributions to a traditional IRA for 2025, you may be able to take an IRA deduction."), so the reply requires answers the source does not need to determine the result. No value differs. The candidate refusals are other forms' refusals reached through its worksheet for persons with no contribution (scveh low_use, s179.multi, s1.alimony_unsupported; refused returns) and its se_screen placements (classm.f5695.2 and classm.ssb.5 on completed returns); the project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (ira.both_kinds, ira.kbh_short, person.ira_over_limit, ira.over_comp, ira.special_gate, person.ira_dist_gate on stage16.refuse.*; p590a p. 35). Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/person.json; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,014 partial records; every witness listed by a scratch rerun of tools/transcription/triage.mjs with the witness cap lifted): 2,450 of 4,724 comparisons agree; 10 project-refuses, 270 candidate-refuses, 1,994 candidate-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 3 candidate-refuses, 901 candidate-unknown.

Ruling: model error; f8606 (2025) Part I: "In 2025, did you take a distribution from a traditional IRA, or make a Roth IRA conversion?" Same expression as fix round 3 apart from form: its Form 8606 filer test and a distribution; its filer test reads person.ira_nondeductible, so for a person who made no traditional contribution it still needs the worksheet's answers (ira_basis, ira_dist, ira_work_plan, ira_excess, plan, plan_screen and se_screen). All 805 candidate-unknown records on completed returns are persons who answered sav_trad no (checked on each witness in a scratch rerun), where the interview does not ask those questions: an answer the source does not need. No value differs. The candidate refusals are other forms' refusals reached through its worksheet for persons with no contribution (scveh low_use, s179.multi, s1.alimony_unsupported; refused returns) and its se_screen placements (classm.f5695.2 and classm.ssb.5 on completed returns); the project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (ira.both_kinds, ira.kbh_short, person.ira_over_limit, ira.over_comp, ira.special_gate, person.ira_dist_gate on stage16.refuse.*; p590a p. 35). Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/person.json; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,014 partial records; every witness listed by a scratch rerun of tools/transcription/triage.mjs with the witness cap lifted): 2,743 of 4,724 comparisons agree; 10 project-refuses, 270 candidate-refuses, 1,701 candidate-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 3 candidate-refuses, 805 candidate-unknown.

Ruling: model error; Form 8606 (2025), Part I: with no 2025 distribution, "Enter the amount from line 3 on line 14". As before the reply writes line 14 as its nondeductible amount plus its Form 8606 line 2 (line 3) and refuses a distribution at its ira_dist_gate, the same two amounts as the project's, but through those lines it still needs the worksheet and basis answers for a person who made no traditional contribution (ira_basis, ira_basis_adjusted, ira_dist, ira_work_plan, ira_excess, plan, plan_screen and se_screen). Of the candidate-unknown records on completed returns, checked on each witness in a scratch rerun, 899 are persons who answered sav_trad no, where the interview does not ask those questions (classm.f2441.1.deepseek, classm.f8995.2, stage15.f8880.s2) (the other 26 are contributors, stage16.ira.i1 to i10, asked plan, plan_screen, se_screen or eligible_educator where the interview does not ask them: placement); for such a person the amount is zero whatever the answers (i1040gi p. 95, Line 20: "If you made contributions to a traditional IRA for 2025, you may be able to take an IRA deduction."), so the reply requires answers the source does not need to determine the result. No value differs. The candidate refusals are other forms' refusals reached through its worksheet for persons with no contribution (scveh low_use, s179.multi, s1.alimony_unsupported; refused returns) and its se_screen placements (classm.f5695.2 and classm.ssb.5 on completed returns); the project refusals are the cited Pub. 590-A refusals (ira.both_kinds, ira.kbh_short, person.ira_over_limit, ira.over_comp, ira.special_gate, person.ira_dist_gate on stage16.refuse.*; p590a p. 35). Fix round 4 re-check: the only project change since fix round 3 is BUG-0259 (person.itin_applied now counts an application to renew an expired ITIN), which this line does not read. Ruling: model error; i5329 p. 1, Who Must File: "You must file Form 5329 if any of the following apply." The list includes "The contributions for 2025 to your traditional IRAs, Roth IRAs, Coverdell ESAs, Archer MSAs, HSAs, or ABLE accounts exceed your maximum contribution limit", besides the early-distribution cases; "However, if distribution code 1 is correctly shown in box 7 of all your Forms 1099-R and you owe the additional tax on the full amount shown on each Form 1099-R, you don't have to file Form 5329." The project's line is one for a person who must file (f5329.must: an exception claimed, a code S distribution, or an HSA excess contribution, f5329.l48), read by s2.line8_must and so by the Schedule 2 line 8 box (s2.line8_box, "If not required, check here"). The reply counts early distributions with a code other than 1 or an exception and leaves excess contributions out (its note: "filing because of excess contributions is left to Form 5329"). On 125 records of completed returns the person's only reason is an HSA excess contribution (f5329.l48 above zero; classm.f8889.1 and .1.deepseek spouse, classm.f8880.3 and .3.deepseek, classm.s2.2, classm.f5329.1, generated 163, 174 and 238): project 1, candidate 0, and in the candidate's graph the printed Schedule 2 line 8 box is checked (True against False on 119 of them, the other 6 unknown through other lines of the reply), telling the IRS no Form 5329 is required when the 6% HSA excess tax on that line needs one. The 3 other completed records (stage21.blind.e3, e4, e5: project 1, candidate 2, 4 and 3) count distributions rather than persons; only a count above zero is read, so they move nothing. The project refusals are cited Form 8889 screens (f8889 mfs, allocation_bad, unsupported, bad_months) and retirement.wrong_kind on refused returns; the candidate refusal is retirement screen_refused on stage21.refuse.early_screen, which the project refuses on the same screen. On stage21.blind.e4 and generated 236 (completed, no SIMPLE distribution: x_l1_s $0.00; e5 and mut.e23_over_cap now fall into the reply's x_prior09 unknown, ruled at person.x_limited_sum) it is True against False, so in the candidate's graph f5329 refuses returns the project computes; what it lacks is the project's scope decision, which no IRS page states and which the prompt withholds (the helper is given by id and type only). Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit: "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11: "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return"; i8863 p. 2: "You may be able to claim an education credit if you, your spouse, or a dependent you claim on your tax return was a student enrolled at or attending an eligible educational institution." The reply changed: its student gate now accepts person.tin or person.itin_applied, as the project's does, so fix round 3's TIN kind (an ITIN applied for and issued) is gone. Fix round 4 re-check: BUG-0259 rewords person.itin_applied to count an application to renew an expired ITIN; both sides read the answer in the same place, so the change moves both alike and creates no difference between them, and it is consistent with the deeming quoted (a renewal is filed as an application on Form W-7). It still omits f8863.paid, the return-level screen that precedes person.edu, so every candidate-unknown record on a completed return but one is a return with f8863.paid no, where the interview never asks the student's answers (edu and edu_over; classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5, checked on each witness in a scratch rerun): an answer the source does not need (the other is a partial of classm.f8863.2.deepseek withholding edu_over for a person the project does not need it for). The worksheet arithmetic is the project's aqee definition; no value differs. Lines 27 to 30 also omit the filers' TIN test (household.all_tin, amb.2025.edu_filer_tin, owner decision D7(a)); no record isolates it. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/person.json; 1,390 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,014 partial records; every witness listed by a scratch rerun of tools/transcription/triage.mjs with the witness cap lifted): 2,372 of 4,724 comparisons agree; 2,352 candidate-unknown; on returns the full interview completes: 926 candidate-unknown.

Second transcription's note: Open: Form 8863 sends a student without an EIN only implicitly to the lifetime learning credit. Chosen: the American opportunity requirements fail and the student's expenses go to line 31, because that credit does not require the EIN.

Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit: "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)"; p970 p. 11: "If an ATIN or ITIN is applied for on or before the due date of a 2025 return (including extensions) and the IRS issues an ATIN or ITIN as a result of the application, the IRS will consider the ATIN or ITIN as issued on or before the due date of the return"; i8863 p. 2: "You may be able to claim an education credit if you, your spouse, or a dependent you claim on your tax return was a student enrolled at or attending an eligible educational institution." The reply changed: its student gate now accepts person.tin or person.itin_applied, as the project's does, so fix round 3's TIN kind (an ITIN applied for and issued) is gone. Fix round 4 re-check: BUG-0259 rewords person.itin_applied to count an application to renew an expired ITIN; both sides read the answer in the same place, so the change moves both alike and creates no difference between them, and it is consistent with the deeming quoted (a renewal is filed as an application on Form W-7). It still omits f8863.paid, the return-level screen that precedes person.edu, so every candidate-unknown record on a completed return but one is a return with f8863.paid no, where the interview never asks the student's answers (edu and edu_over; classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5, checked on each witness in a scratch rerun): an answer the source does not need (the other is a partial of classm.f8863.2.deepseek withholding edu_over for a person the project does not need it for). The amount (institution-paid expenses less lines 2a to 2c, floored at zero) is the project's aqee ws4_llc; no value differs. The candidate refusal is its edu_over declaration on stage19.refuse.period_over_person, which the project refuses on the same screen. | Step 2 | person.rrta, person.se_screen | person.rrta, person.se_screen |
| Step 3 | person.rrta, person.se_screen | person.rrta, person.se_screen |
| Step 5 | person.rrta, person.se_screen | person.rrta, person.se_screen |
| Step 7 | person.rrta, person.se_screen | person.rrta, person.se_screen |
| Step 21 | person.rrta, person.se_screen | person.rrta, person.se_screen |
| 27 | person.h_edu_over_refused | person.h_edu_over_refused |
| 28 | person.h_edu_over_refused | person.h_edu_over_refused |
| 29 | person.h_edu_over_refused | person.h_edu_over_refused |
| 30 | person.h_edu_over_refused | person.h_edu_over_refused |
| 31 | person.h_edu_over_refused | person.h_edu_over_refused |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: person.employers
    printed: "employers"
    label: "Number of employers (Forms W-2) for this person"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one}
  - id: person.overcount
    printed: "overcount"
    label: "Number of employers that withheld more than $10,918.20 of social security tax"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one, where: {op: test, eq: [w2.over, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.over
    printed: "over"
    label: "Any one employer withheld more than $10,918.20"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [person.overcount, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
  - id: person.wages
    printed: "wages"
    label: "Total wages for social security (Forms W-2, boxes 3 and 7)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.wages}
  - id: person.withheld
    printed: "withheld"
    label: "Total social security tax withheld (Forms W-2, box 4)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box4}
  - id: person.eligible
    printed: "eligible"
    label: "More than one employer, total wages of more than $176,100, and no one employer withheld more than $10,918.20"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [person.employers, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.wages, {param: ss.base}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.over, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.excess
    printed: "excess"
    label: "Social security tax withheld in excess of $10,918.20"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.withheld, take: {param: ss.limit}}
  - id: person.zero
    printed: "zero"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: person.credit
    printed: "credit"
    label: "Excess social security tax withheld (Schedule 3, line 11), figured separately for each spouse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible, {const: true}]}, then: person.excess}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.rrta]
  - id: person.sep_s1
    printed: "Step 1"
    label: "Enter your net profit from Schedule C (Form 1040), line 31"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.sc_total}
    note: "Open: Pub. 560 counts only the business that has the plan when there is more than one business, and the supplied inputs do not identify that business. Chosen: all of this person's Schedule C results."
  - id: person.sep_s2
    printed: "Step 2"
    label: "Enter your deduction for self-employment tax from Schedule 1 (Form 1040), line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.se_line13}
  - id: person.sep_s3
    printed: "Step 3"
    label: "Net earnings from self-employment. Subtract step 2 from step 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.sep_s1, take: person.sep_s2}
    note: "The worksheet prints no floor. The declared result type is nonnegative, so a net loss is entered as zero."
  - id: person.h_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent, used to express the whole-percent plan rate as a money ratio"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}
  - id: person.h_hundred
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One hundred cents, used to express the whole-percent plan rate as a money ratio"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.hundred_cents}
  - id: person.h_sep_part
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Plan contribution rate in whole percent, expressed in cents"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: person.h_cent, count: person.sep_rate}
  - id: person.h_sep_whole
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Plan contribution rate plus 100, expressed in cents (rate worksheet, line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_sep_part, person.h_hundred]}
  - id: person.sep_s5
    printed: "Step 5"
    label: "Multiply step 3 by step 4 (the rate from the Rate Table for Self-Employed)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: person.sep_s3, part: person.h_sep_part, whole: person.h_sep_whole, places: 6}
    note: "No table for the Rate Table for Self-Employed is supplied. Its Column B equals rate / (100 + rate) rounded to six places, so step 4 is expressed that way inside this line."
  - id: person.h_comp_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Annual compensation limit, $350,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.comp_limit}
  - id: person.sep_s6
    printed: "Step 6"
    label: "Multiply $350,000 by your plan contribution rate (not the reduced rate)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: person.h_comp_limit, part: person.h_sep_part, whole: person.h_hundred, places: 2}
  - id: person.sep_s7
    printed: "Step 7"
    label: "Enter the smaller of step 5 or step 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_s5, person.sep_s6]}
  - id: person.sep_s8
    printed: "Step 8"
    label: "Contribution dollar limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.sep_dollar_limit}
  - id: person.sep_s21
    printed: "Step 21"
    label: "This is your maximum deductible contribution (no elective deferrals: the smaller of step 7 or step 8)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_s7, person.sep_s8]}
    note: "Steps 9 to 20 are skipped because elective deferrals to the plan are outside the tool (SARSEP and qualified plans are screened by person.plan_screen)."
  - id: person.h_edu_student
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Expenses were paid for this person as a student, the Form 1098-T requirement is met, and the student has a TIN by the due date"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.tin, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 23 to 26 lead to lines 27 through 30, the institution's EIN is available, and filers and student have TINs"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_student, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This student goes to line 31 (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_student, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total adjustments: tax-free educational assistance received in 2025 and 2026 and refunds"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_a2025, person.edu_a2026, person.edu_refunds]}
  - id: person.h_edu_over_refused
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "An academic period's assistance and refunds were more than that period's expenses paid to the institution"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_over, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses. Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line27}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
  - id: person.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line28}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
  - id: person.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line29}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
  - id: person.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: line30}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
  - id: person.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: person.edu_inst, mat: person.edu_mat, adj: person.h_edu_adj}, line: ws4_llc}
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_llc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
    note: "Open: Form 8863 sends a student without an EIN only implicitly to the lifetime learning credit. Chosen: the American opportunity requirements fail and the student's expenses go to line 31, because that credit does not require the EIN."
  - id: person.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: person.one}
  - id: person.h_educator_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Maximum educator expense deduction per person, $300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: s1.educator_max}
  - id: person.h_educator_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified educator expenses, up to $300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.educator_expenses, person.h_educator_max]}
  - id: person.educator_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Educator expenses this eligible educator can deduct (Schedule 1, line 11)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_educator_capped}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.eligible_educator, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.hsa_forms
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms 8889 for this person"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one}
  - id: person.hsa_split
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of this person's Forms 8889 that divide a family contribution limit between spouses (line 6)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.one, where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.hsa_agreed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of this person's Forms 8889 dividing the family limit by a different agreed allocation"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f8889
      field: f8889.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [f8889.allocation, {const: "agreed"}]}
  - id: person.hsa_step1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Refigured family contribution limit (Form 8889 line 6 instructions, Step 1)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.step1, where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.hsa_share
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This spouse's allocated share of the family contribution limit (Form 8889 line 6 instructions, Step 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.share, where: {op: test, eq: [f8889.split, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.hsa_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "HSA deduction (Form 8889, line 13)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line13}
  - id: person.hsa_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxable HSA distributions (Form 8889, line 16)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line16}
  - id: person.hsa_addtax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional 20% tax (Form 8889, line 17b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.line17b}
  - id: person.tip_employers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of employers on the Qualified Tips From More Than One Employer Worksheet"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.one}
  - id: person.tip_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of the qualified tips worksheet, column (d)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.col_d}
  - id: person.tip_reported
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of the qualified tips worksheet, column (b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: tipjob, field: tipjob.amount}
  - id: person.ot_employers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of employers on the Qualified Overtime Compensation From More Than One Employer Worksheet"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: otjob, field: otjob.one}
  - id: person.ot_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of the qualified overtime worksheet, column (b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: otjob, field: otjob.col_b}
  - id: person.box1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box1}
  - id: person.box2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from Form(s) W-2, box 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box2}
  - id: person.ira_amount_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA distributions entered on Form 1040, line 4a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.amount_a, where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.ira_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxable IRA distributions entered on Form 1040, line 4b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.taxable, where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.ira_rollovers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of IRA distributions with a rollover (Form 1040, line 4c, box 1)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.plan_amount_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Pensions and annuities entered on Form 1040, line 5a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.amount_a, where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}}
  - id: person.plan_taxable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxable pensions and annuities entered on Form 1040, line 5b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.taxable, where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}}
  - id: person.plan_rollovers
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of pension or annuity distributions with a rollover (Form 1040, line 5c, box 1)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.sav_dist
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions received in 2025 counted on Form 8880, line 4"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.sav_amount}
  - id: person.medicare5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Medicare wages and tips from Form W-2, box 5"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box5}
  - id: person.medicare6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Medicare tax withheld from Form W-2, box 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box6}
  - id: person.sc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedules C for this person"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one}
  - id: person.ho_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of this person's businesses claiming a deduction for business use of the home"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one, where: {op: test, eq: [sc.ho_uses, {const: true}]}}
    note: "The interface name is not defined by the IRS pages. Read from the Schedule C instructions on more than one business qualifying for the home deduction."
  - id: person.mq_q4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Depreciable bases of MACRS property placed in service during the last 3 months of the tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.mq_q4}
  - id: person.mq_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Depreciable bases of MACRS property placed in service during the entire tax year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.mq_total}
  - id: person.cost179
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total cost of section 179 property placed in service (Form 4562, line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.cost179}
  - id: person.s179_nonlisted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elected section 179 cost of property other than listed property (Form 4562, line 6, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.f6}
  - id: person.s179_listed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elected section 179 cost of listed property (Form 4562, line 29)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.f29}
  - id: person.income179
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Business income from this person's Schedules C figured without the section 179 deduction (Form 4562, line 11)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.line31_pre179}
    note: "The Form 4562 line 11 instructions are not in the supplied pages. Chosen from the interface and field names: Schedule C line 31 before section 179."
  - id: person.sc_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Net profit or (loss) from Schedule C, line 31, all of this person's businesses"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.result}
  - id: person.se_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 6: add lines 4c and 5b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line6}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employment tax (Schedule SE, line 12)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line12}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.se_line13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Deduction for one-half of self-employment tax (Schedule SE, line 13)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line13}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.h_se_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 3"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line3}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.h_se_net
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule SE, line 3, minus the deduction on Schedule 1, line 15"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.h_se_line3, take: person.se_line13}
  - id: person.h_simple_comp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE compensation: net earnings from self-employment, Schedule SE, line 4, before subtracting SIMPLE contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: se, bind: {line2: person.sc_total, line8a: person.wages}, line: line4a_pos}}
    refuses: [person.se_screen, person.rrta]
  - id: person.h_simple_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE IRA salary reduction contribution limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ret.simple_limit}
  - id: person.h_simple_catchup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE IRA catch-up contribution limit for this person's age"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: simple_catchup, keys: [person.simple_age]}
  - id: person.h_simple_total_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contribution limit plus catch-up limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_simple_limit, person.h_simple_catchup]}
  - id: person.h_simple_def_limited
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions, limited to the dollar limits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.simple_deferral, person.h_simple_total_limit]}
  - id: person.h_simple_def_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions, limited also to compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_def_limited, person.h_simple_comp]}
  - id: person.h_simple_match_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "3% of compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_comp, rate: {param: ret.match_rate}}
  - id: person.h_simple_match_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Matching limit: dollar for dollar on salary reduction contributions, up to 3% of compensation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_match_cap, person.h_simple_def_ded]}
  - id: person.h_simple_comp_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Compensation taken into account for nonelective contributions, up to $350,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_simple_comp, person.h_comp_limit]}
  - id: person.h_simple_ne_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "2% nonelective contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_simple_comp_capped, rate: {param: ret.nonelective_rate}}
  - id: person.h_simple_er_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Employer contribution limit under the plan's formula"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.simple_kind, {const: "match"}]}, then: person.h_simple_match_limit}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.simple_kind, {const: "nonelective"}]}, then: person.h_simple_ne_limit}
  - id: person.h_simple_er_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Employer contributions for yourself, limited to the formula"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.simple_employer, person.h_simple_er_limit]}
  - id: person.h_simple_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Deductible SIMPLE IRA contributions for yourself"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_simple_def_ded, person.h_simple_er_ded]}
  - id: person.h_sep_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Your actual SEP contribution, not to exceed your maximum deductible contribution"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_contrib, person.sep_s21]}
  - id: person.h_plan_refused
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "This person contributes to a SEP or SIMPLE IRA plan and a situation outside the tool applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.plan_screen, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.s16
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed SEP and SIMPLE plan deduction (Schedule 1, line 16)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "sep"}]}, then: person.h_sep_ded}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.h_simple_ded}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.h_plan_refused]
  - id: person.positive_profits
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of all net profits from Schedule C, line 31 (Form 7206, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.profit_pos}
  - id: person.sehi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed health insurance deduction (Form 7206, line 14)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.f7206_line14}
  - id: person.qbi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified business income or (loss) from this person's Schedules C (Form 8995, line 1(c))"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.qbi}
  - id: person.eic_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Worksheet B: self-employment earnings (line 1e, or line 2c when Schedule SE is not required)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.h_se_net}
    note: "Worksheet B line 1e is Schedule SE line 3 less line 13, and line 2c is the Schedule C result. These agree here because line 13 is zero when no self-employment tax is owed and lines 1a, 1b, 4b and 5a are screened out."
  - id: person.sc_1a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of this person's businesses with qualified tips or overtime to be deducted on Schedule 1-A"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.one, where: {op: test, eq: [sc.tips_ot, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.dc_benefits
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependent care benefits for Form 2441, line 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.dc_benefits}
  - id: person.h_box10_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of this person's Forms W-2 with an amount in box 10"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.one, where: {op: test, eq: [w2.has_box10, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.dc_has_box10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person has dependent care benefits in box 10 of a Form W-2"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.h_box10_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: person.dc_earned_b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for Form 2441, lines 18 and 19 (not including dependent care benefits)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.box1, person.h_se_net]}
  - id: person.h_dc_1e
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Taxable dependent care benefits on Form 1040, line 1e, attributed to this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.dc_has_box10, {const: true}]}, then: f1040.line1e}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.dc_has_box10, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    note: "Open: Form 1040 line 1z is a return-level amount, and the instructions do not attribute line 1e between spouses. Chosen: line 1e goes to the person with box 10 benefits."
  - id: person.dc_earned
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income for Form 2441, lines 4 and 5: this person's part of Form 1040, line 1z, plus Schedule SE, line 3, minus the deduction on Schedule 1, line 15"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.dc_earned_b, person.h_dc_1e]}
  - id: person.sav_ineligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person was born after January 1, 2008, is claimed as a dependent on someone else's 2025 return, or was a student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_student, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.sav_line1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional and Roth IRA contributions for 2025 (Form 8880, line 1)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_contrib, person.sav_roth]}
    refuses: [person.sav_able]
  - id: person.h_sav_w2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals shown on Forms W-2, box 12"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.sav_deferral}
  - id: person.h_sav_simple
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Salary reduction contributions to this person's self-employed SIMPLE IRA"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.simple_deferral}
        - {guard: {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}, then: person.zero}
  - id: person.sav_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals to a 401(k) or other qualified employer plan, voluntary employee contributions, and 501(c)(18)(D) plan contributions for 2025 (Form 8880, line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_sav_w2, person.sav_voluntary, person.h_sav_simple]}
    refuses: [person.sav_excess]
  - id: person.ira_contrib
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional IRA contributions for 2025 (IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 11)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: true}]}, then: person.ira_trad}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: false}]}, then: person.zero}
    refuses: [person.ira_excess]
  - id: person.ira_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Covered by a retirement plan at work or through self-employment (SEP or SIMPLE)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "none"}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_line2_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 2 amount for the filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2}
  - id: person.h_ira_line2_nc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 2 amount for the person who wasn't covered by a plan, $246,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2_not_covered}
  - id: person.ira_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 2, this person's column"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_line2_nc
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_line2_base
    note: "The worksheet's $89,000 for a separate filer who lived apart all year has no separate parameter. The status parameter gives the amount for a separate filer who lived with the spouse; the lived-apart case is left to the form-level gate on the IRA worksheet."
  - id: person.ira_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 6: subtract line 5 from line 2, this person's column (zero if line 5 is not less than line 2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.ira_line2, take: ira.line5}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit50
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$8,000 if age 50 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit_50}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The applicable amount: $7,000 if under age 50 at the end of 2025, $8,000 if age 50 or older"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit50}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_limit}
  - id: person.h_ira_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This column uses the $10,000 range: single, head of household, married filing separately, or the IRA of a joint filer who wasn't covered"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "qss"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_cov, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.h_ira_full_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_narrow}
  - id: person.h_ira_full_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$20,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_joint}
  - id: person.h_ira_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 result at which the applicable amount is entered on line 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}, then: person.h_ira_full_narrow}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}, then: person.h_ira_full_joint}
  - id: person.h_ira_m70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 70% (0.70)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_70}}
  - id: person.h_ira_m80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 80% (0.80)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_80}}
  - id: person.h_ira_m35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 35% (0.35)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_35}}
  - id: person.h_ira_m40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 40% (0.40)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.ira_line6, rate: {param: ira.rate_40}}
  - id: person.h_ira_product
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by the percentage that applies"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_m70
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_m80
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_m35
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_age50, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_m40
  - id: person.h_ira_rounded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result isn't a multiple of $10, increase it to the next multiple of $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_product, unit: 1000, rule: up}
    note: "Open: an exact product with a fraction of a cent could round to a multiple of $10 before this step. Chosen: the product is rounded to the cent first, because no atom applies an upward unit directly to a product."
  - id: person.h_ira_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.min}
  - id: person.h_ira_floored
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result is $200 or more, enter the result. But if it is less than $200, enter $200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_rounded, person.h_ira_min]}
  - id: person.ira_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 7, this person's column"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit_amount
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, le: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}
          then: person.zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, ge: [person.ira_line6, person.h_ira_threshold]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit_amount
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_line6, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, lt: [person.ira_line6, person.h_ira_threshold]}
          then: person.h_ira_floored
  - id: person.h_ira_7_10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The smaller of line 7 or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.ira_line7, ira.line10]}
  - id: person.ira_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 12: the smallest of line 7, 10, or 11, this person's column"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_7_10, person.ira_contrib]}
  - id: person.h_ira_10_11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The smaller of line 10 or line 11 of the IRA Deduction Worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [ira.line10, person.ira_contrib]}
  - id: person.ira_nondeductible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 1: subtract line 12 of the worksheet from the smaller of line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_10_11, take: person.ira_deduction}
  - id: person.ira_files_8606
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person must file Form 8606: nondeductible contributions for 2025, or a distribution or conversion with basis in traditional IRAs"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_basis, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.ira_8606_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 2: your total basis in traditional IRAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_basis}
    refuses: [person.ira_basis_adjusted]
  - id: person.ira_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This person files Form 8606 and took a traditional IRA distribution or made a Roth IRA conversion in 2025 (Form 8606, lines 4 to 13)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_files_8606, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.ira_8606_line14
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 14: with no distribution or conversion, the amount from line 3 (lines 1 and 2 added)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_nondeductible, person.ira_8606_line2]}
    refuses: [person.ira_dist_gate]
  - id: person.h_ira_ssb_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The smaller of the applicable contribution amount or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_limit_amount, ira.line10]}
  - id: person.ira_ssb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction used on the Social Security Benefits Worksheet, line 6, when no one is covered by a retirement plan"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_ssb_a, person.ira_contrib]}
    note: "The social security worksheet may not be used with traditional IRA contributions when a plan covers the taxpayer or spouse (screened by ssb.pub915). This line therefore figures the deduction without IRA Deduction Worksheet lines 2 to 6 and does not depend on total income."
  - id: person.ira_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 9: net earnings from self-employment minus any deductions on Schedule 1, lines 15 and 16. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_se_net, take: person.s16}
  - id: person.edu_kid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8863, line 7 conditions 1 and 2: under 18, or 18 or a full-time student under 24 with earned income less than one-half of support, and at least one parent alive"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_parent, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.sav_born, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2007, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2007, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_born_2002, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_fulltime, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}
    note: "The joint-return condition is not per person and is left to Form 8863."
  - id: person.x_l1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 5329, line 1: early distributions includible in income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l1}
  - id: person.x_l1_s
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part of line 1 that is a distribution from a SIMPLE IRA in the first 2 years"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l1_s}
  - id: person.x_l2_s
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part of line 2 that is a distribution from a SIMPLE IRA in the first 2 years"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_s}
  - id: person.h_x_e05
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions under exception 05, medical expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e05}
  - id: person.h_x_e05_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception 05 distributions, up to the unreimbursed medical expenses minus 7.5% of AGI (Schedule A, line 4)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x_e05, sa.line4]}
  - id: person.x_c05
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amount excepted under exception 05, medical expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, gt: [person.h_x_e05, {const: 0}]}, then: person.h_x_e05_capped}
        - {guard: {op: test, le: [person.h_x_e05, {const: 0}]}, then: person.zero}
    note: "Open: the exception's limit is a return-level amount. Chosen: each person is limited to Schedule A line 4, leaving the combined check to the return-level gate."
  - id: person.h_x_e09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions under exception 09, first home"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e09}
  - id: person.h_x_cap09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "First home limit, $10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_first_home}
  - id: person.h_x_room09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000 reduced by all prior qualified first-time homebuyer distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_x_cap09, take: person.x_prior09}
  - id: person.h_x_c09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amount excepted under exception 09, first home"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x_e09, person.h_x_room09]}
  - id: person.h_x_e19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions under exception 19, birth or adoption"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e19}
  - id: person.h_x_cap19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Birth or adoption limit, $5,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_birth}
  - id: person.h_x_room19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$5,000 for each birth or adoption"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: person.h_x_cap19, count: person.x_births}
  - id: person.h_x_c19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amount excepted under exception 19, birth or adoption"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x_e19, person.h_x_room19]}
  - id: person.h_x_e22
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions under exception 22, domestic abuse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e22}
  - id: person.h_x_cap22
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Domestic abuse victim distribution limit, $10,300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_abuse}
  - id: person.h_x_c22
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amount excepted under exception 22, domestic abuse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x_e22, person.h_x_cap22]}
  - id: person.h_x_e23_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of distributions under exception 23, emergency personal expenses"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.one, where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_e23, {const: true}]}}
  - id: person.x_two23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "More than one emergency personal expense distribution in the calendar year"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [person.h_x_e23_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
  - id: person.h_x_e23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions under exception 23, emergency personal expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2_e23}
  - id: person.h_x_cap23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Emergency personal expense distribution limit, $1,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_emergency}
  - id: person.h_x_c23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amount excepted under exception 23, emergency personal expenses"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x_e23, person.h_x_cap23]}
    refuses: [person.x_two23]
    note: "Only one such distribution per calendar year qualifies, and the entries do not show which came first. Chosen: refuse when there are two or more."
  - id: person.x_limited_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total excepted under the exceptions with limits (05, 09, 19, 22 and 23)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.x_c05, person.h_x_c09, person.h_x_c19, person.h_x_c22, person.h_x_c23]}
  - id: person.x_other_exc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total excepted under all other exceptions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.l2
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_e05, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_e09, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_e19, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_e22, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_e23, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.x_l2_all
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 5329, line 2: early distributions included on line 1 that are not subject to the additional tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.x_limited_sum, person.x_other_exc]}
  - id: person.h_x_l2_raw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amounts the exceptions cover, before the per-person limits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: retirement, field: retirement.l2}
  - id: person.x_limit_binds
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A limit on an exception reduced the amount excepted"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [person.h_x_l2_raw, person.x_l2_all]}
  - id: person.x_hsa_excess
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 5329, line 47: excess contributions to HSAs for 2025, including excess employer contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f8889, field: f8889.excess_amt}
  - id: person.x_tax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional taxes from Form 5329 included on Schedule 2 (Form 1040), line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f5329, field: f5329.tax}
    refuses: [person.x_other]
  - id: person.x_must
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of early distributions requiring Form 5329: code 1 with an exception, or a code other than 1"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, gt: [retirement.l1, {const: 0}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, ne: [retirement.code, {const: "early_1"}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_exception, {const: true}]}
    note: "Form 5329 need not be filed when code 1 is correctly shown on all Forms 1099-R and the additional tax is owed on the full amount. This count covers distributions only; filing because of excess contributions is left to Form 5329."
```
